lam_st_record_effect = {
	save_scope_as = lam_st_record_head
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = lam_fac_rec
			exists = var:lam_fac_rec_title
		}
		var:lam_fac_rec_title = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:lam_st_record_head.var:lam_fac_rec = 1
					has_variable = lam_gn1_y0
				}
				lam_st_record_slot_effect = {
					N = 1
					KEY = $KEY$
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:lam_st_record_head.var:lam_fac_rec = 2
					has_variable = lam_gn2_y0

				}

					N = 3

				}

				limit = {

lam_st_record_seed_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st1_k
			}
			has_variable = lam_gn$N$_ev
			has_variable = lam_gn$N$_evy
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = lam_gn$N$_st1_k
			value = var:lam_gn$N$_ev
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = lam_gn$N$_st1_y
			value = var:lam_gn$N$_evy
		}
	}
}
lam_st_record_slot_effect = {
	lam_st_record_seed_effect = {
		N = $N$
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					var:lam_gn$N$_st1_k ?= flag:$KEY$
					var:lam_gn$N$_st1_m ?= lam_now_m
					var:lam_gn$N$_st1_a ?= scope:lam_st_record_head
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st5_k
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn$N$_st6_k
				value = var:lam_gn$N$_st5_k
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st6_k
			}
			remove_variable = lam_gn$N$_st6_k
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st5_y
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn$N$_st6_y
				value = var:lam_gn$N$_st5_y
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st6_y
			}
			remove_variable = lam_gn$N$_st6_y
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st5_m
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn$N$_st6_m
				value = var:lam_gn$N$_st5_m
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st6_m
			}
			remove_variable = lam_gn$N$_st6_m
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st5_a
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn$N$_st6_a
				value = var:lam_gn$N$_st5_a
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st6_a
			}
			remove_variable = lam_gn$N$_st6_a
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st4_k
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn$N$_st5_k
				value = var:lam_gn$N$_st4_k
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st5_k
			}
			remove_variable = lam_gn$N$_st5_k
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st4_y
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn$N$_st5_y
				value = var:lam_gn$N$_st4_y
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st5_y
			}
			remove_variable = lam_gn$N$_st5_y
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st4_m
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn$N$_st5_m
				value = var:lam_gn$N$_st4_m
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st5_m
			}
			remove_variable = lam_gn$N$_st5_m
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st4_a
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn$N$_st5_a
				value = var:lam_gn$N$_st4_a
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st5_a
			}
			remove_variable = lam_gn$N$_st5_a
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st3_k
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn$N$_st4_k
				value = var:lam_gn$N$_st3_k
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st4_k
			}
			remove_variable = lam_gn$N$_st4_k
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st3_y
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn$N$_st4_y
				value = var:lam_gn$N$_st3_y
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st4_y
			}
			remove_variable = lam_gn$N$_st4_y
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st3_m
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn$N$_st4_m
				value = var:lam_gn$N$_st3_m
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st4_m
			}
			remove_variable = lam_gn$N$_st4_m
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st3_a
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn$N$_st4_a
				value = var:lam_gn$N$_st3_a
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st4_a
			}
			remove_variable = lam_gn$N$_st4_a
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st2_k
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn$N$_st3_k
				value = var:lam_gn$N$_st2_k
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st3_k
			}
			remove_variable = lam_gn$N$_st3_k
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st2_y
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn$N$_st3_y
				value = var:lam_gn$N$_st2_y
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st3_y
			}
			remove_variable = lam_gn$N$_st3_y
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st2_m
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn$N$_st3_m
				value = var:lam_gn$N$_st2_m
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st3_m
			}
			remove_variable = lam_gn$N$_st3_m
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st2_a
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn$N$_st3_a
				value = var:lam_gn$N$_st2_a
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st3_a
			}
			remove_variable = lam_gn$N$_st3_a
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st1_k
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn$N$_st2_k
				value = var:lam_gn$N$_st1_k
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st2_k
			}
			remove_variable = lam_gn$N$_st2_k
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st1_y
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn$N$_st2_y
				value = var:lam_gn$N$_st1_y
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st2_y
			}
			remove_variable = lam_gn$N$_st2_y
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st1_m
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn$N$_st2_m
				value = var:lam_gn$N$_st1_m
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st2_m
			}
			remove_variable = lam_gn$N$_st2_m
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st1_a
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn$N$_st2_a
				value = var:lam_gn$N$_st1_a
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn$N$_st2_a
			}
			remove_variable = lam_gn$N$_st2_a
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = lam_gn$N$_st1_k
			value = flag:$KEY$
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = lam_gn$N$_st1_y
			value = current_year
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = lam_gn$N$_st1_m
			value = lam_now_m
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = lam_gn$N$_st1_a
			value = scope:lam_st_record_head
		}
	}
}
lam_st_record_shift_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:lam_gn_cut <= 1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn2_st1_k
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn1_st1_k
				value = var:lam_gn2_st1_k
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn1_st1_k
			}
			remove_variable = lam_gn1_st1_k
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn2_st1_y
			}
			set_variable = {

		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = lam_gn2_st1_m

			set_variable = {
				name = lam_gn1_st1_m
				value = var:lam_gn2_st1_m
			}
		}

				value = var:lam_gn2_st1_a
